# TRIED: every idea this project has tried, merged from the sessions' parts (NewIdeas keeps it current)
Check here before proposing or screening an idea. Line shape: idea | wall | line | exact | verdict | the number that decided it | date | source | [sessions]. Same-ID ideas from several sessions are merged; every source kept.
Parts: leap 382, bigmoe 193, evalsuite 45, breakthrough 154, moonshots 375, fit12 154, newideas 140, mtphead 98, orchestrator 26; merged ideas 1467.
ADOPTED / PASS levers end in 'novelty: published / adapted / new / engineering (prior art)' from novelty.tsv; the checks and sources are in NOVELTY.md.
